\displaystyle+\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}f^{\varepsilon}\Big]-\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big]+\frac{\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}]}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}\Big[\partial_{t}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}-\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Big]\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big]+\varepsilon^{k-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}[\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},f^{\varepsilon})+\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})\big]+\varepsilon^{k}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big]

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{k}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}f^{\varepsilon}\Big]

(4.39)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\Big)\cdot\frac{\big(v-u\big)}{2RT}f^{\varepsilon}\Big]+\varepsilon^{k}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathcal{Q}_{0}.

Furthermore, taking the inner product of
4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}
and (
4.2
) with
|\alpha|=1
, one gets

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|[\nabla_{x}E_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}],4\pi RT\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle

(4.40)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;\big|\big\langle\partial_{t}T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\Big[\frac{v-u}{T}\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{|\alpha|=1}\big|\big\langle v\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\left[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[f^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big)\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},f^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})\big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\Big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\Big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{Q}_{0},T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

For the second term on the L.H.S. of (
4.40
), applying (
2.11
) and (
1.31
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}],4\pi RT\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3\delta}{4\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}-\frac{C\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{C\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\big(\varepsilon\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\big).

We now turn to estimate the R.H.S. of (
4.40
) separately.
Using (
1.31
), one sees that
the first three terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\nabla_{x}\rho\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{x}u\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}T\|_{\infty}\Big)\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\Big).

It follows from Lemma
4.3
that the 4th, 9th and 11th terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
) can be dominated by

\displaystyle C\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big]\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{1}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\Big(\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{D}}\Big)\Big].

By Lemma
4.2
, we see that the 5th, 6th, 10th and 12th terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle C\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big]\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{(2-j)}}\|\nabla_{x}^{j}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\Big).

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
),
using (
2.4
), (
3.18
) and Sobolev’s inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}|_{D}|f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}+|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}|f^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\Big)\Big(|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]|_{D}+|\Lbrack\nabla_{x},{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}\Rbrack[f^{\varepsilon}]|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{k-1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\Big(\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\nabla_{x}{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\Big(\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\nabla_{x}{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\epsilon_{1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|\Big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\Big).

For the 8th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
), note that
0\leq t\leq\varepsilon^{-1/3}
,
by virtue of (
4.1
), (
2.4
) and Cauchy’s inequalities, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},f^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})\big],T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)\big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{1}}+\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{1}_{D}}\big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\Lbrack\nabla_{x},{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}\Rbrack[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{1-p_{0}}\big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|+\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{D}\big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}.

For the last term on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
),
(
4.1
) gives

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{Q}_{0},T\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|.

Finally, plugging the above estimates into (
4.40
) and multiplying the resulting inequality by
\varepsilon
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big[\varepsilon\Big(

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big]+\delta\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

(4.41)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon\Big((1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big)\Big[\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\Big(\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{D}}\Big)\Big]+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+2}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k+1}(1+t)^{2k}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|.

Step 3. Estimates on the second order derivative of the remainders.

In this step, we proceed to derive the estimate of
\|[\nabla^{2}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{2}E_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{2}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\|
. For results in this direction, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big[\varepsilon^{2}

\displaystyle\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big]+\delta\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

(4.42)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2}\Big((1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big)\Big[\|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{6}}\right)\Big(\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{D}}\Big)\Big]+\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon^{2k+3}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k+2}(1+t)^{2k}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|.

To prove (
4.42
), we first take the inner product of
4\pi RT\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}
and (
4.2
) with
|\alpha|=2
to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}],4\pi RT\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle

(4.43)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;\big|\big\langle\partial_{t}T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=2}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\Big[\frac{v-u}{T}\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=2}\big|\big\langle v\partial^{\alpha}\big(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{2}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[f^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big)\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},f^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})\big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\Big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\Big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{Q}_{0},T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

Using (
1.31
) and (
2.11
) again, we see that the 2nd term on the L.H.S. of (
4.43
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}],4\pi RT\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3\delta}{4\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}-\frac{C\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{1}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{C\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\varepsilon\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\Big).

It follows from (
1.31
) that the first three terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\nabla_{x}\rho\|_{W^{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{x}u\|_{W^{1,\infty}}+\|\nabla_{x}T\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\Big)\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\Big).

In view of Lemma
4.3
, one sees that the 4th, 9th and 11th terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
) are no more than

\displaystyle C\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big]\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\Big(\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{D}}\Big)\Big].

By Lemma
4.2
,
we see that the 5th, 6th, 10th and 12th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle C\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big]\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{(3-j)}}\|\nabla_{x}^{j}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\Big).

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
), applying (
2.13
), (
4.37
) and Sobolev’s inequalities, we get that

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}|_{D}|f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}+|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}|f^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\Lbrack\nabla_{x}^{2},{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}\Rbrack[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{k-1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{1}_{D}}+\|{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{1}_{D}}\Big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\Big(\|\nabla^{2}_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\nabla^{2}_{x}{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\epsilon_{1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{1}_{D}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}\Big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\varepsilon\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\Big).

For the 8th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
),
(
2.4
) and (
4.1
) lead us to

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},f^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})\big],T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)\big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{2}}+\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|\Lbrack\nabla_{x}^{2},{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}\Rbrack[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{1-p_{0}}\big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{1}}+\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{1}_{D}}\big)\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{1}_{D}}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}.

For the last term on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
), one gets from (
4.1
) that

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{Q}_{0},T\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|.

Then Substituting the above estimates in (
4.43
) and multiplying the resulting inequality by
\varepsilon^{2}
give (
4.42
).

Finally, (
4.1
) follows from (
4.2
), (
4.41
) and (
4.42
), this ends the proof of Proposition
4.1
.
∎

As what we have done in Section
3
, in order to close the estimate (
4.1
) constructed in Proposition
4.1
, one further needs to deduce the
H^{2}
estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
which is determined by (
4.22
) and (
4.23
). However, compared to the case of the Landau equation (
1.1
), the estimate of
h^{\varepsilon}
for the VML system (
1.2
) is more complicated. Firstly, the velocity growth terms such as
\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}
cannot be dominated by the degenerate dissipation of the linearized Landau collision operator at large velocity, to overcome this difficulty, a time-dependent exponential weight
\exp\left(\frac{(1+|v|^{2})}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)
, which leads to extra dissipation term, is introduced so that these velocity growth can be eliminated. Secondly, in order to handle the terms like
-\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}
, we also design a polynomial velocity weight function
\langle v\rangle^{\ell-i}
with
i
being the order of
x-
derivatives to compensate the weak dissipation of the linearized Landau operator. Nevertheless, this polynomial velocity weight also cause velocity growth while estimating the nonlinear collision operator
\varepsilon^{k-1}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon})
. Fortunately, these growths can be absorbed by the exponential weight in the sense that

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[{\langle v\rangle}^{i}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}(1+|v|^{2})}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)\Big]\lesssim\Big(\frac{\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}{\epsilon_{1}}\Big)^{\frac{i}{2}}.

(4.44)

Before estimating
h^{\varepsilon}
, we give some useful lemmas which will be used later, the first one is concerned with the velocity growth caused by the electric field.

Lemma 4.4
.

Assume that
f^{\varepsilon},E_{R}^{\varepsilon},B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and
h^{\varepsilon}
are smooth solutions of the Cauchy problem (
4.20
), (
4.21
), (
4.22
) and (
4.23
) for the VML system (
1.2
) and satisfy (
4.24
), then
for
i=0,1,2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}{\bf 1}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+{\bf 1}_{i\geq 1}\varepsilon\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{6}}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}{\bf 1}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}}.

(4.45)

Proof.

For brevity, we only verify (
4.45
), the other two inequalities can be treated similarly.
To this end, by (
1.31
), one has for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{3}}
that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}(1+t)^{n}\Big(\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon(1+t)\Big(\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{6}}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}}.

Thus the proof of Lemma (
4.4
) is finished.
∎

The following Lemma is devote to the control of the velocity growth caused by Lorentz force.

Lemma 4.5
.

Assume that
f^{\varepsilon},E_{R}^{\varepsilon},B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and
h^{\varepsilon}
are smooth solutions of the Cauchy problem (
4.20
), (
4.21
), (
4.22
) and (
4.23
) for the VML system (
1.2
) and satisfy (
4.24
), then
for
i=0,1,2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\Big(Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}_{D}}\Big),

(4.46)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\varepsilon\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}_{D}},

(4.47)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\big[\big(E_{n}

\displaystyle+v\times B_{n}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{n}\big],w_{i}^{2}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

(4.48)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{6}}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}_{D}}\Big).

Proof.

We will prove (
4.46
) and (
4.47
) and omit the proof of (
4.48
) for brevity.
To prove (
4.46
), from (
1.31
) and Lemma
2.2
, it follows that

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big],w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=i}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\big|\big\langle E\cdot\nabla_{v}\big(w^{2}_{i}\big)\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=i}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\big|\big\langle\big(v\times\partial^{\alpha}B\big)\cdot\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\|E\|_{W^{i,\infty}}+\|B\|_{W^{i,\infty}}\Big)\Big[\big\|\sqrt{1+|v|}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{-1}w_{i}\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{i-1}}+\Big\|\frac{|v|}{{\langle v\rangle}}w_{i}\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}]\Big\|_{H^{i-1}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}_{D}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\Big(Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i-1}_{D}}\Big).

For (
4.47
), one has

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=i}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\big(w^{2}_{i}\big)\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=i}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\big|\big\langle\big(v\times\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=i}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{-1}w_{1}\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{3}_{x}}\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{|\alpha+\alpha^{\prime}|=i}{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\Big\|\frac{|v|}{{\langle v\rangle}}w_{i}\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}]\Big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{3}_{x}}\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

Furthermore, by Lemma
2.2
, for
|\alpha^{\prime}|<i
and
1\leq i\leq 2
, we can get that

\displaystyle\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{-1}w_{i}\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{3}_{x}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{v}}{\langle v\rangle}^{-2}w_{i}^{2}|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}|^{2}_{L^{3}_{x}}dv

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{v}}\Big({\langle v\rangle}^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{i}|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{6}_{x}}\Big)\Big({\langle v\rangle}^{-\frac{1}{2}}w_{i}|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|w_{i-1}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha^{\prime}|}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}\left\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha^{\prime}|+1}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D},

and similarly,

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{|v|}{{\langle v\rangle}}

\displaystyle w_{i}\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}]\Big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{3}_{x}}^{2}\leq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{v}}w_{i}^{2}|\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}]|^{2}_{L^{3}_{x}}dv

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{v}}\Big({\langle v\rangle}^{-\frac{1}{2}}w_{i}|\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}]|_{L^{6}_{x}}\Big)\Big({\langle v\rangle}^{-\frac{1}{2}}w_{i-1}|\big({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{v})[\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}]|_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big)dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|w_{i-1}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha^{\prime}|}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}\left\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha^{\prime}|+1}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}.

Noting that
(1+t)^{-\beta}\lesssim Y(t)
and
\varepsilon\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}Y(t)
for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-1/3}
, we thus conclude that

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{i}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\Big)\Big(\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+{\bf I}_{i\geq 1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i-1}_{D}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\varepsilon\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}_{D}}.

This ends the proof of Lemma
4.5
.
∎

We now state our main result on the weighted energy estimates on
h^{\varepsilon}
as follows.

Proposition 4.2
.

Assume that
f^{\varepsilon},E_{R}^{\varepsilon},B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and
h^{\varepsilon}
are smooth solutions of the Cauchy problem (
4.20
), (
4.21
), (
4.22
) and (
4.23
) for the VML system (
1.2
) and satisfy (
4.24
), then it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}

\displaystyle\big(\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}+i}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\big)+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}+i}Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\delta\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}+i}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}

(4.49)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}+i}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{3}+i}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}\Big)+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}+i}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{3}+i}\Big((1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}^{2}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}_{D}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{2k+\frac{7}{3}+i}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{k+\frac{4}{3}+i}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

Proof.

The proof is divided into three steps.

Step 1. Basic weighted energy estimate of
h^{\varepsilon}
.
In this step, we deduce the weighted
L^{2}
estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
. For results in this direction, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}

\displaystyle\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\big)+\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}Y\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}

(4.50)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)+\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+\frac{7}{3}}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{4}{3}}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

To prove this,
we first take the
L^{2}
inner product of
w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}
with (
4.22
) and use (
2.19
), to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+Y\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

(4.51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\Big|\Big\langle\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})],w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\frac{E_{i}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\mathcal{Q}_{1},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

We now turn to estimate the right hand side of (
4.51
) term by term.
For the 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
),
(
2.17
) directly gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}.

For the 3rd term on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
), from (
1.32
),
it follows that
{\langle v\rangle}w_{0}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}
is bounded,
therefore, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).

Since
\varepsilon\ll\epsilon_{1}
, by Lemma
4.5
, we see that the 4th, 8th and 10th terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\epsilon_{1}Y\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).

For the 5th, 9th and 11th terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
), using Lemma
4.4
, one sees that they share the same upper bound
C\epsilon_{1}Y\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}
.

For the 6th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
),
we get from (
2.15
) and Sobolev’s inequality that

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}\Big)|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\,

(4.52)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\Big(\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}\Big).

Here we have used Sobolev’s inequality
\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\|f\|_{H^{2}_{x}}
to bound both
\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}
and
\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}
, because either
|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}
or
|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}
cannot bear more
x-
derivative.
Furthermore, using (
4.44
), one has

\displaystyle\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[{\langle v\rangle}^{2}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}(1+|v|^{2})}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)\Big]\big\|wh^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\epsilon_{1}}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}},

(4.53)

and similarly

\displaystyle\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[{\langle v\rangle}^{2}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}(1+|v|^{2})}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)\Big]\big\|wh^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\epsilon_{1}}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}.

(4.54)

Moreover, for
k\geq 3
, it follows

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{i}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-2-\frac{i}{2}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{1-\frac{i}{2}}

(4.55)

according to (
4.37
).

Thus, in view of
t\leq\varepsilon^{-1/3}
and the assumption
\varepsilon\ll\epsilon_{1}
, we get by plugging (
4.53
), (
4.54
) and (
4.55
) into (
4.2
) that

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\epsilon_{1}}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{1}{\epsilon_{1}^{2}}\ln^{2}(\mathrm{e}+t)\varepsilon^{3}\big\|wh^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}.

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
),
(
4.1
) and Lemma
2.5
give

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})],w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}[\varepsilon(1+t)]^{i-1}(1+t)\Big(\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|+\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}\Big)\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

Similarly, for the last term on the R.H.S. of (
4.51
), we have

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\mathcal{Q}_{1},w^{2}_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

Putting the above estimates into (
4.51
) and multiplying the resulting inequality by
\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}
yield (
4.50
).

Step 2. First order derivative estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
with weight.

In this step, we continue to deduce the estimate of
\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|
. Applying
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2)
to (
4.22
) yields

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\frac{\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)}{RT}\cdot\big(v-u\big)\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big]

\displaystyle+\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big]-\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big]+\frac{\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}[h^{\varepsilon}]}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}]}{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon})+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}[\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big]-\varepsilon^{k}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big]

(4.56)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big(\frac{E_{i}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)+\varepsilon^{k}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathcal{Q}_{1}.

Next, taking the
L^{2}
inner product of
w^{2}_{1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}
and (
4.2
) with
|\alpha|=1
and applying (
2.19
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

(4.57)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big[\big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i}\Big],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{E_{i}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big),w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{Q}_{1},w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

We now turn to estimate the R.H.S. of (
4.40
) individually.
For the 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
), by (
2.17
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\Big(\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}+\|w_{1}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}\Big).

For the 3rd term on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
), direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}+C\varepsilon\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\Big).

Applying Lemma
4.4
, we can bound the 4th, 9th and 11th terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
) by

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big]\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

For the fifth, eighth and tenth term on the R.H.S. of (
4.40
),
assuming
\varepsilon\ll\epsilon_{1}
and using Lemma
4.5
, we see that they enjoy the same upper bound

\displaystyle C\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\Big)+\epsilon_{1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{D}}.

For the 6th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.57
),
we get from (
2.15
) and Sobolev’s inequalities tha

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}\Big)|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\,

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}|w_{1}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|w_{1}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}\Big)|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\Big(\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}\Big).

Next, similar to (
4.53
) and (
4.54
), one has

\displaystyle\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[{\langle v\rangle}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}(1+|v|^{2})}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)\Big]\big\|wh^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}}{\sqrt{\epsilon_{1}}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-1}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[{\langle v\rangle}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}(1+|v|^{2})}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)\Big]\big\|wh^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}}{\sqrt{\epsilon_{1}}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}.

These estimates together with (
4.55
) leads us to

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}}{\sqrt{\epsilon_{1}}}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}{\epsilon_{1}}\varepsilon^{2}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\varepsilon\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2},

where (
4.55
),
t\leq\varepsilon^{-1/3}
and
\varepsilon\ll\epsilon_{1}
are used again.

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.57
),
from (
4.1
) and Lemma
2.5
, we get for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-1/3}
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})],w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}[\varepsilon(1+t)]^{i-1}(1+t)\Big(\|w_{1}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{D}}\Big)\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big(\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}\Big).

Similarly, for the last term on the R.H.S. of (
4.57
), one has

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}\mathcal{Q}_{1},w^{2}_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

Putting the above estimates into (
4.57
) and multiplying the resulting inequality by
\varepsilon^{7/3}
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{3}}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\big)+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{3}}Y\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}

(4.58)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{8}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\Big)+\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{3}}\Big((1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|w_{0}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+\frac{10}{3}}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{7}{3}}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{1}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

Step 3. Second order derivative estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
with weight.

In this final step, we proceed to deduce the estimate of
\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|
.
For results in this direction, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}}

\displaystyle\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\big)+\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}}Y\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{3}}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}

(4.59)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{3}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{11}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}}\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{D}}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+\frac{13}{3}}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{10}{3}}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

To show this, we first take the
L^{2}
inner product of
w^{2}_{2}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}
and (
4.2
) with
|\alpha|=2
and use (
2.19
) to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{1}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}

(4.60)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E_{i}+v\times B_{i}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{i}\Big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big(\frac{E_{i}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big),w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{Q}_{1},w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

We now turn to estimate the R.H.S. of (
4.60
) term by term.
For the 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
4.60
), by (
2.17
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{D}}.

The 3rd term on the R.H.S. of (
4.60
) can be directly bounded by

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{2}\partial_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\Big).

For the 4th, 9th and 11th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
),
in light of Lemma
4.4
, we can bound them by

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big]\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}.

Assuming
\varepsilon\ll\epsilon_{1}
and using Lemma
4.5
, we see that the fifth, eighth and tenth terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.43
) share the same bound

\displaystyle C\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\epsilon_{1}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{D}}.

For the 6th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.60
),
applying (
2.15
), (
4.55
) and Sobolev’s inequalities, one has

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-2}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-2}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}\Big)|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\,

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}|w_{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|w_{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}\Big)|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\,

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-2}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{L^{2}}|w_{2}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}+|w_{2}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}\big|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell-2}\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}\big|_{D}\Big)|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}|_{D}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big\|w_{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{H^{2}}\|w_{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}^{2}.

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
4.60
),
by (
4.1
) and Lemma
2.5
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\big|\big\langle[\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i},h^{\varepsilon})+\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{i})],w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}[\varepsilon(1+t)]^{i-1}(1+t)\Big(\|w_{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}+\|w_{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{D}}\Big)\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big(\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{D}}\Big).

Similarly, for the last term on the R.H.S. of (
4.60
), it follows

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathcal{Q}_{1},w^{2}_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|w_{2}\nabla_{x}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\|.

Submitting these estimates in (
4.60
) and multiplying the resulting inequality by
\varepsilon^{10/3}
give (
4.59
).

Finally, (
4.49
) follows from (
4.50
), (
4.58
) and (
4.59
), this ends the proof of Proposition
4.2
.
∎

4.3.
Proof of the Theorem
1.2

We are now ready to complete the Proof of Theorem
1.2
. For brevity, we only derive the energy estimates (
1.37
) and omit details of the proof of non-negativity proof of
F^{\varepsilon}
since it is
similar to the proof given in
[
51
,
52
]
.

For this purpose, combing Propositions
4.2
and
4.1
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i}\Big[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{i}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}+\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}Y\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i+\frac{1}{3}}\Big[\|\nabla_{x}^{i}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\Big(\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}+\varepsilon(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big)\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}\Big)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{\frac{10}{3}}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{D}}+\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i}\Big((1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big)\Big[\|\nabla_{x}^{i}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\Big(\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}}+\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}_{D}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i}\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{i}f^{\varepsilon}\|+\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|\Big),

where
\mathcal{E}(t)
is defined in (
1.38
).

Next, choosing
\varepsilon_{0}>0
sufficiently small such that

C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}}{8RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+\mathcal{D}(t)\lesssim\Big[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}+(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\Big]\mathcal{E}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2},

where
\mathcal{D}(t)
is given by (
1.39
). Then for
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, we apply Gronwall’s inequality over
t\in[0,\varepsilon^{-1/3}]
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}(s)\,ds\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0)+1.

This completes the proof of Theorem
1.2
.
